	playfram 			0x00
	wait 				1
	goto              	AthuzelonLocal00

AthuzelonWalking:
	playfram          	0x180
	wait              	1
	playfram          	0x1A0
	wait              	1
	playfram          	0x1C0
	wait              	1
	playfram          	0x1E0
	setvertpos        	0
AthuzelonGlowLoop:
	wait              	3
	spruluselo        	1213 0 0 # Athuzelon Glow (cm\f-p\athuzelon-glow.grp)
	goto              	AthuzelonGlowLoop
	
AthuzelonWalkingToIdle:
	playfram          	0x1C0
	wait              	1
	playfram          	0x1A0
	wait              	1
	playfram          	0x180
	wait              	1
	goto				AthuzelonLocal00

AthuzelonWarpIn:
	imgol             	2515 0 0 # Athuzelon Warp Flash (cm\f-p\athuzelon.grp)
	goto              	AthuzelonWalkingToIdle
